# Goblin Pet
Categories: Legendary items, Pets, More info needed, Mining pets, 0.20
[Goblin Pet]
Type: [[Mining]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1x [[Ugly Fossil]] 512,000x (8,000 stacks (512,000 items total)) Rough Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: GOBLIN
Tooltip: Mining Pet; Magic Find: +0.07➡7; Ore Fortune: +1➡100; Grunt Work Gain +2.5➡250 Mining Speed when mining Ores.; Fetid Thief Gain +1➡100 Mining Spread while in the Mines of Divan.; Free-range Eggs Increases the chance of finding rare goblin eggs by 0.5➡50%.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; LEGENDARY
The Goblin Pet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mining]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
The Goblin Pet can be forged in [[The Forge]] with 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Amber Gemstone]] and 1 [[Ugly Fossil]] for 7 days after donating [[Ugly Fossil]] to [[Dr. Stone]]. It requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Goblin Pet]] 1 Goblin Pet
  - 1 [[Ugly Fossil]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Amber Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Amber Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Amber Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Amber Gemstone]]

# Godseed
Aliases: God Seed, God seed
Categories: Legendary items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Godseed]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GODSEED
Tooltip: A seed so powerful, it feels like it shouldn't exist. Its growth knows no limits.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; LEGENDARY MUTATION
A Godseed is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Godseed can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if every positive crop effect is present.
While growing, a Godseed needs water and has 40 Growth Stages. When placed in the [[Greenhouse]], all adjacent crops receive all positive effects.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Godseeds also drop all [[Crops (disambiguation)|Crops]] : 117 (1 stack plus 53) [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]], 117 [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]], 209 (3 stacks plus 17) [[Pumpkin]], 246 (3 stacks plus 54) [[Wheat]], 369 (5 stacks plus 49) [[Cactus]], 492 (7 stacks plus 44) [[Cocoa Beans]], 492 [[Moonflower]], 492 [[Sunflower]], 492 [[Sugar Cane]], 492 [[Wild Rose]], 738 (11 stacks plus 34) [[Nether Wart]], 738 [[Potato]], 862 (13 stacks plus 30) [[Carrot]], 985 (15 stacks plus 25) [[Melon Slice]], and +12,800 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Godseed can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 500 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Godseed grants an additional 500 [[Copper]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sun's Grasp]]|None|1x [[Ray of Helios]] [[Godseed]] 3x Godseed 3x [[All-in Aloe]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Compacted Sunflower]]|Recipe: Godseed ×3 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x64 ×2 + [[Ray of Helios]] + [[All-in Aloe]] ×3 → [[Sun's Grasp]]|
## Gallery
- Godseed in the Greenhouse Godseed in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Notes
- Godseed cannot be used to fully meet the requirements for another Godseed, at least 1 other non-Godseed crop is required.
  - [[Wild Rose]], [[Thornshade]] or [[Witherbloom]] — thanks to their Effect Spread ability — suffice, if they're adjacent to another Godseed.
- On top of the requirement of every positive crop effect; the said effects need to be highest tier. Yield and Farming XP boost need to be +30% and water retention at +100%.
## Tips
- Players can place a [[Snoozling]] in Godseed's designated area to see if it meets the requirements.
- Using [[Shadevine]], [[Thornshade]], [[Gloomgourd]], [[Cocoa Beans]] and [[Mushroom|Red or Brown Mushroom]], the requirements can be met without needing to water the plants.
- Godseed and [[Jerryflower]] are the only [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mutations]] not required for [[Rose Dragon Pet]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Godseed.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Godseed.|

# Gold Bottle Cap
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Endcap Enchantments
[Gold Bottle Cap]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Junker Joel]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOLD_BOTTLE_CAP
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; When applied to a fishing rod, increases its Luck of the Sea level by 1!; Can be applied once.; Requires Luck of the Sea VI!; One man's trash is, indeed, another man's treasure.; LEGENDARY
A Gold Bottle Cap is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrade]] that can be applied to any [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] with [[Luck of the Sea|Luck of the Sea VI]] to upgrade it to [[Luck of the Sea|Luck of the Sea VII]].
## Obtaining
A Gold Bottle Cap can be purchased from [[Junker Joel]] for 512 (8 stacks) [[Rusty Coin]], 64 (1 stack) [[Busted Belt Buckle|Busted Belt Buckles]], and 8 [[Old Leather Boot|Old Leather Boots]]. It can also be obtained as a reward from [[Experiments|Superpairs]].
## Usage
A Gold Bottle Cap can be applied in an anvil to any [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] with [[Luck of the Sea|Luck of the Sea VI]] to upgrade it to [[Luck of the Sea|Luck of the Sea VII]].
## History

Gold Gift Talisman is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that grants the [[Player]] +25% [[Coins|Coin]] and [[Skills|EXP]] rewards from [[Gifts]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Purple Gift Talisman]] and is the final accessory in the "Gift Talisman" line.

### Purchase
The Gold Gift Talisman can be purchased from [[Einary]] at [[Einary's Emporium]] in [[Jerry's Workshop]].
Note: This item is purchasable only during December of real life. When attempting to buy it outside the period, the player will get a "Only sold in December!" error message. This applies to all lower tier purchases too.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOLDEN_BALL
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Bountiful reforge when combined with a Farming Tool.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Farming Fortune  Speed; Bountiful Bonus Earn +0.2 Coins every time you harvest a Crop.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 30.; LEGENDARY REFORGE STONE
A Golden Ball is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Bountiful]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Farming Tools]]. It grants [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Speed]].
## Obtaining
A Golden Ball can be bought from [[Jake]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]] after bringing him a [[The Loch Emperor|Loch Emperor]]. Since a [[The Loch Emperor|Loch Emperor]] can only be caught on [[Galatea]], a necromancy item such as a [[Summoning Ring]], [[Necromancer Sword]], or [[Reaper Scythe]] must be used to summon it at [[Jake's House]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Golden Ball can be combined with a [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Bountiful]] reforge, to grant [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Speed]], which scale with the Farming Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXX (30)]]. A Farming Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Bountiful]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Bountiful Bonus, which grants 0.2 [[Coins]] per harvested crop.

### [[The Alchemist's Staff]] requires giving [[Golden Ball]] 1 Golden Ball to [[Baar]] or the [[Alchemage]].
### Kuudra Teeth Plaque
[[Golden Ball]] 1 Golden Ball can be given to [[Drakuu]] after obtaining the [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] for an [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque|Exquisite Kuudra Teeth Plaque]].
## Trivia
- After only one player finding the Golden Ball after one month of being in the game, 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Dueces gave out information about it.
  - A large portion of the community attempted to find it, putting over a 400,000,000 [[Coins]] bounty on it.
  - Almost one month later, on May 20th, 2021, Dueces said that a list of methods attempted included the actual obtaining method. Shortly after, the method of obtaining the Golden Ball became public, as a few were put on the Auction House, and the NBT data of the item showed "entity_required": "SKELETON_EMPEROR".
    - [[Sea Creatures]] had been crossed off early on as they all despawn before reaching Jake's House.
- Golden Ball and [[Flowering Bouquet]] are the only reforge stones that can be consumed for a purpose different than applying a reforge.
- The [[Reforging|Bountiful]] reforge does not immediately grant players coins for each crop gained. Instead, the player's purse is updated once every 30 seconds.

A Golden Belt is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Belts|Belt]].
## Obtaining
A Golden Belt can be claimed from [[Shen Magnum Opus]] for completing the Fifth Sequence in the [[Cerebral Citadel]].
## Usage
Belt:
Golden Belt [[Intelligence]]: +10 [[Speed]]: +4 [[Rift Time]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Gravity Falls; Increases your total Rift Time by 5% but it goes down 3% faster.; Monster melee attacks reduce 1 less Rift Time.]
## Trivia
- With the +5% [[Rift Time]] and 3% faster drain rate, this result in roughly +1.94% more [[Rift Time]]
- The ability of the Golden Belt is a reference to the popular show Gravity Falls.

A Golden Bounty is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrade]] that can be applied to any [[Weapons|Weapon]] with [[Scavenger|Scavenger V]] to upgrade it to [[Scavenger|Scavenger VI]].
## Obtaining
A Golden Bounty can be bought from [[Liz]] for 20 [[Stock of Stonks]]. It can also be obtained as a reward from [[Experiments|Superpairs]].
Golden Bounty can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
A Golden Bounty can be applied to any weapon with [[Scavenger|Scavenger V]] in an anvil in order to upgrade it to [[Scavenger|Scavenger VI]].

The Golden Dragon Pet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] pet obtained from purchasing it in a structure in [[Mithril Deposits]] within [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Obtaining
A Level 1 [[Golden Dragon Pet|Golden Dragon Egg]] can be purchased from the Dragon residing in the [[Dragon's Lair (Crystal Hollows)|Dragon's Lair]] located in [[Mithril Deposits]] of [[Crystal Hollows]]. The Dragon guards 3 eggs located next to a loot chest. The Golden Dragon Egg can be purchased by clicking one of the eggs. The cost of the Golden Dragon Egg is as follows:
- 1 [[Golden Dragon Pet|Golden Dragon Egg]]
  - 500,000,000 (7,812,500 stacks) [[Coins]]
  - 50 [[Enchanted Gold Block]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Amethyst Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Jade Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Sapphire Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Amber Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Topaz Gemstone]]
There's no limit on the amount of eggs bought per lobby.
In total, the Golden Dragon Egg costs at current [[Bazaar]] prices, or if the player forges the Perfect Gemstones themselves.
## Usage
Both the Egg and the Pet have the type of [[Combat]] Pet. From level 1 to level 99, the Golden Dragon Egg has no stats nor abilities, and cannot hold [[Pet Items]].
Upon reaching level 100, it will hatch into a Golden Dragon Pet and gain its stats and perks.

## Trivia
- The Golden Dragon Pet can give a maximum of +250% [[Damage]] if the player's bank contains a minimum of 1,000,000,000 [[Coins]].
- 50 [[Magic Find]] is required to max Dragon's Greed.
- 100,000,000 (1,562,500 stacks) [[Gold Ingot|Gold collection]] is required to max out Shining Scales.
- The damage bonus is capped at 1,000,000,000 [[Coins]], despite the maximum balance of a player's bank being 60,000,000,000 [[Coins]] for a [[Bank/UI|Palatial Account]].
  - If it were uncapped, the damage buff would go up to +15000% [[Damage]].
- The Legendary Treasure perk is additive, not multiplicative.
- The Shining Scales perk adds to the base stats of the pet. This makes it the best [[Magic Find]] pet.
- Purchasing a [[Golden Dragon Pet|Golden Dragon Egg]] will reward the player with the [[Achievements|"The Dragon's Egg" Achievement]].
- It is one of the few pets to have 4 abilities (5 when at lvl 200) at [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity.
- The Golden Dragon Pet's Legendary Treasure Perk works with all types of damage, including magic damage and damage that cannot be increased through normal means such as [[Precursor Eye]]'s eye beam.
- When equipped with the [[Ancient Golden Dragon Skin]], the pet will display cosmetic levels, the levels gained from XP gained beyond what is required for level 200. Getting these cosmetic Pet Level ups will send a message in chat as normal.
- On a [[Co-op]] profile, the Shining Scales perk uses the player's personal Gold collection, but the Legendary Treasure perk uses the co-op bank account's balance.
- Before the 0.26 update, the Gold's Power ability worked on some items that are not weapons, specifically the [[Treecapitator]], [[Cocoa Chopper]] and [[Pumpkin Dicer]]. On the other hand, it does not work on [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] with 5 Perfect [[Gemstone|Gemstones]], [[Cactus Knife]] or [[Fungi Cutter]].
  - It even worked on [[Jerry-chine Gun]].
- Symbiosis does not provide extra [[Coins]] from a second Golden Dragon.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Golden Dragon Pet.|
|Aug 20, 2024|The Golden Dragon Pet's Shining Scales perk now caps out at 100M Gold collection rather than 1B. It provides the same buff 1B Gold collection previously did.|
|Feb 6, 2025|The Gold's Power ability now works on Blaze Slayer daggers while they're in Auric mode.|
|Jul 3, 2026|The Gold's Power ability now increases the potency of [[Midas' Sword]] and [[Midas Staff]]'s Greed Ability.|
|Oct 15, 2026|Removed Golden Dragon Pet's [[Magic Find]] from it's base stats and from it's Shining Scales ability.|
|Oct 15, 2026|Increased Golden Dragon Pet's Shining Scales ability's [[Strength]] from +11.1 [[Strength]] per digit to +15 [[Strength]] per digit.|

|Feb 2, 2022|Shining Scale's [[Strength]] and [[Magic Find]] boost are now shown as base stats to the pet.|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Shining Scales is now capped at 1,000,000,000 Gold ([[Gold Ingot]]) collection (10 digits).|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Dragon's Greed's +0.5% [[Strength]] per 5 [[Magic Find]] is now capped at 100 [[Magic Find]].|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Golden Dragon Eggs will now show if pet candy has been used on them.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Max bonus for Golden Dragon Pet's Shining Scales ability is now gained at 100,000,000 collection, rather than 1,000,000,000.|
|Unknown Date|The Gold's Power ability now works on Enderman Slayer katanas while the Soulcry ability is active.|

|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|The Gold's Power ability now increases the potency of [[Midas' Sword]] and [[Midas Staff]]'s Greed Ability by 10%, instead of granting 100 [[Strength]] to golden weapons.|

## Trivia
- While its debuff of -4 [[Intelligence]] might sound unattractive at first, it still grants [[Accessory Power]], and it can be [[Enrichments|Enriched]].
- To craft it, a total of 125 (1 stack plus 61) [[Green Jerry Talisman|Green Jerry Talismans]] (or an equivalent number of [[Blue Jerry Talisman|Blue Jerry Talismans]] or [[Purple Jerry Talisman|Purple Jerry Talismans]]) must be combined.
- On its release, it was believed that this was obtained from [[Jerry Box|Golden Jerry Boxes]]. This was later proven false.

## Obtaining
The Golden Monkey Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on January 13, 2021. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 750 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 2,000 purchases.

The Golden Monkey Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Monkey Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

The Grizzly Bear Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Tough Claws]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Grizzly Bear Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Grizzly Bear]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Fusion
A Grizzly Bear Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Grizzly Bear Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Tough Claws]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Tough Claws ([[Attributes]]); Whenever you cut down a Tree, reduce its [[Toughness]] by +0.1–1.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Consecutive throws cost 2x more mana and deal 2x more damage than the previous. (max 16x); Mana Cost: 20; This item can be reforged!; ☠ Requires Zombie Slayer 8.; LEGENDARY SWORD
The Halberd of the Shredded is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VIII (8)]], upgrades from the [[Reaper Falchion]], and is the final form of the [[Undead Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Zombie Slayer VIII (8) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Reaper Falchion]] 4x [[Shredded Sinew]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Viscera]] x64 ×4 + [[Shredded Sinew]] ×4 + [[Reaper Falchion]] → Halberd of the Shredded|
## Usage
Sword:
Halberd of the Shredded
[Effects]
[Heal 25 Health per hit.; Deal +250% damage to Undead (Undead (Mob Type)) mobs.; Gain +45 Combat Wisdom against Undead (Undead (Mob Type)) mobs. Ability: Throw RIGHT CLICK; Throw your Halberd, damaging all enemies in its path, dealing 10% melee damage. Consecutive throws cost 2x more Mana and deal 2x more damage than the previous. (max 16x)]
The Halberd of the Shredded deals +250% damage to [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] mobs, heals 25 [[Health]] on hit, and grants +45 [[Combat Wisdom]] against [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] mobs.
The Halberd of the Shredded's Throw ability allows the player to throw their halberd. The thrown halberd damages all enemies in its path, dealing 10% of its melee damage and activating all of its passive bonuses: consecutive throws deal 2x damage and cost 2x more [[Mana]] than the previous, up to a 16x multiplier. The ability costs 20 [[Mana]].
Unlike other melee weapons that have ranged abilities like the [[Livid Dagger]], the Throw ability is treated as a melee strike, causing it to have the same effects and calculations of a melee hit. As such, [[Enchantments]] like [[Thunderbolt]], [[Fire Aspect]], [[Lethality]], etc. will trigger. The 25 [[Health]] healing on hit and pet abilities will also activate.
The Throw ability ignores invincibility frames, causing it to hit multiple times per target. This is noticeable on mobs with large hitboxes, such as [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]], the [[Magma Boss]], and [[Sadan|Sadan's Giants]].
## Essence Crafting
The Halberd of the Shredded can be upgraded with Undead Essence.
Essence Required; [[Undead Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|200|200|
|✪✪|400|600|
|✪✪✪|600|1,200|
|✪✪✪✪|800; 10,000 [[Coins]]|2,000; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|1,000; 25,000 [[Coins]]|3,000; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The Halberd of the Shredded does not give [[Intelligence]] despite its previous two tiers, the [[Revenant Falchion]] and the [[Reaper Falchion]], both doing so.
- When first introduced, the Throw ability would stack more than 16 times, even if the halberd did not hit anything. This allowed players to kill [[Necron]] and other high-health enemies instantly with enough [[Mana]].
- The Halberd of the Shredded, [[Soul Whip]], and [[Flaming Flay]] are the only weapons where sword swapping is still usable.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]

- In-hand model.

The Hazard Control Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Hazard Control Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on September 5, 2026. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 3,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Hazard Control Skin can be applied to a [[Biohazard Armor|Biohazard Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 21 different versions.
- This skin is swappable between 21 hazard control variants!

# Heavy Pearl
Categories: Legendary items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crimson Isle
[Heavy Pearl]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: It can take years for Hellwisp to create a pearl, but the Matriarch secretes so much fluid that it can make multiple of those pearls every day.
Source: [[The Matriarch]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]]); Medium Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]]); Large Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HEAVY_PEARL
Tooltip: It can take years for Hellwisp to create a pearl, but the Matriarch secretes so much fluid that it can make multiple of those pearls every day.; LEGENDARY
The Heavy Pearl is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that can be obtained as a drop by collecting them from [[The Matriarch]] in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Obtaining
### The Matriarch
Heavy Pearls can be obtained by collecting them from [[The Matriarch]]'s stomach, located in the [[Matriarch's Lair]], without getting digested. There are three piles of Heavy Pearls in the [[Belly of the Beast]], which the player must click several times to obtain them. Whilst being in the [[Belly of the Beast]], [[Health]] is set to 100 , and all potion effects are removed. Under normal circumstances, [[Heavy Pearl]] 3 Heavy Pearls can be obtained per real-life day, all of which can be in the same run.
However, by consuming the [[Matriarch's Perfume]], players get to reset [[The Matriarch]] up to a total of four times per real-life day, thus allowing players to obtain up to [[Heavy Pearl]] 15 Heavy Pearls per real-life day. The [[Attributes|Matriarch Cubs]] attribute, obtained from syphoning [[Hellwisp Shard|Hellwisp Shards]], also increases the chance for an additional Heavy Pearl to be collected when obtaining one; when activated, the following text will display.
Your Matriarch Cubs attribute has granted you 1 additional Heavy Pearl!
Dying to the stomach acid, which deals 5 damage per second, will result in a 10m cooldown before another attempt.
### Fishing
The Heavy Pearl can be caught while [[Treasure|Treasure Fishing]] in [[Crimson Isle]]:
|Item|Catch Type|Weight|Pool|
|-|-|-|-|
|9 Heavy Pearl|OUTSTANDING CATCH ([[Treasure]])|50|24.63%|
|3 Heavy Pearl|GREAT CATCH ([[Treasure]])|5|0.34%|
## Usage
Heavy Pearls are primarily used to upgrade Weapons and Armor sets that are upgraded with [[Crimson Essence]]. They are also used in specific Quests.
## Trivia
- There was a period of time when [[The Matriarch]] was inaccessible: during this period the only way to obtain a Heavy Pearl was to again talk to [[Gris]] after completing his quest, or salvage certain Armor pieces.

# Hegemony Artifact
Categories: Legendary items, Accessories, Dark Auction
[Hegemony Artifact]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Tooltip: Accessory Power: +32; Counts for twice the Accessory Power in your Accessory Bag.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; LEGENDARY ACCESSORY
The Hegemony Artifact is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] found in the [[Dark Auction|Darker Auction]], which is only available while the special mayor [[Scorpius]] is in office.
## Obtaining
The Hegemony Artifact can be purchased from the [[Dark Auction|Darker Auction]] when [[Scorpius]] is mayor. It has a minimum price of 100,000,000 [[Coins]].
Hegemony Artifact can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
The Hegemony Artifact has no bonus on its own, but has a special ability where the [[Accessory Power]] of the accessory is doubled. For example, since [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] accessories count for 16 Accessory Power, Hegemony Artifact grants 32. When recombobulated to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it grants 44 Accessory Power instead of 22.
## Trivia
- Before the addition of [[Accessory Power]], the [[Hegemony Artifact]] Hegemony Artifact would double all [[Reforging|Reforge stats]] applied to it.
- [[Enrichments]] are not doubled by this accessory.
- The Hegemony Artifact is one of the most expensive accessories in the game.
- The Hegemony Artifact has the same texture of the skull packs in the Housing menu.
- Alongside [[Bingo Talisman]] and its upgrades, and [[Hocus-Pocus Cipher]] (outside of the [[Rift Dimension]]), it does not grant any effects, which means it can only grant boosts through [[Powers|Accessory Powers]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Apr 15, 2022|The Hegemony Artifact now doubles its [[Accessory Power]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 20, 2020|0.9.8|Added Hegemony Artifact.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Hegemony Artifact now grants double [[Accessory Power]] instead of double Reforge Stats.|

# Helianthus
Aliases: Heli, Heliantus
Categories: Legendary items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Helianthus]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Condensed Helianthus]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 275,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HELIANTHUS
Tooltip: The Crème de la Crop.; Right-click to view recipes!; LEGENDARY
A Helianthus is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item. It is used to craft [[Condensed Helianthus]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
- [[Helianthus]] Helianthus has a chance to drop when breaking a mature [[Moonflower]], [[Sunflower]], or [[Wild Rose]] crop while wearing at least 2 pieces of [[Fermento Armor]] or [[Helianthus Armor]].
- Pieces from different armor sets do not count towards each other's Tiered Bonus.
- The chance can be increased by [[Overbloom]].
- For [[Moonflower]], [[Sunflower]] and [[Wild Rose]], only the block directly broken rolls for [[Helianthus]] Helianthus.
Pieces: Chance
1/4: 0.000% (0 in ∞ crops broken)
2/4: 0.002% (1 in 50,000.0 crops broken)
3/4: 0.003% (1 in 33,333.3 crops broken)
4/4: 0.004% (1 in 25,000.0 crops broken)
### Greenhouse
- [[Helianthus]] Helianthus has a chance to drop when breaking any crop or mutation with a "Harvestable" status inside the [[Greenhouse]] while wearing at least 2 pieces of [[Fermento Armor]] or [[Helianthus Armor]].
- Pieces from different armor sets do not count towards each other's Tiered Bonus.
- The chance can be increased by [[Overbloom]].
Pieces: Chance
1/4: 0.0% (0 in ∞ crops broken)
2/4: 0.8% (1 in 125 crops broken)
3/4: 1.2% (1 in 83.3 crops broken)
4/4: 1.6% (1 in 62.5 crops broken)
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Condensed Helianthus]]|None|[[Helianthus]] 9x Helianthus|Recipe: Helianthus ×9 → [[Condensed Helianthus]]|
### Composter
Helianthus can be used to fill up the [[Composter]]'s Crop Meter by 30k Organic Matter.
## Tips
- When looking to obtain [[Helianthus]] Helianthus for the [[Helianthus Armor]], it is advised to keep using full [[Fermento Armor]] until all 4 pieces can be crafted at once, as replacing parts of it with the Helianthus Armor will reduce the odds for Helianthus to drop.
## Trivia
- In real life, Helianthus is a genus that comprises around 70 species of sunflowers.
- The word Helianthus originates from the Greek word for Sun and the godly personification thereof, Helios.
## History

|Dec 5, 2025|Added Helianthus.|
|Dec 12, 2025|Added Helianthus to the [[Garden Sack]].|
|Dec 12, 2025|Decreased NPC sell price from 300,000 [[Coins]] to 275,000 [[Coins]].|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Helianthus as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Helianthus.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Helianthus as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

## Obtaining
### [[Crystal Nucleus]]
A Helix Fossil has a 1.285297% (1 in 77.8 rolls) chance for each drop in the Crystal Nucleus loot bundle.
## Usage
A Helix Fossil can be used alongside 300,000 [[Coins]] to make an [[Ammonite Pet]] in [[The Forge]] after 3 days.
## Trivia
- This item is likely a reference to the Helix Fossil from the Pokémon series, which is used to gain the Pokémon Omanyte, which is based on ammonites.

The Hellwisp Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Matriarch Cubs]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Hellwisp Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Hellwisp]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Hellwisp Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Matriarch's Lair]].
### Kuudra
A Hellwisp Shard can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Loot]].
### Fusion
A Hellwisp Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Hellwisp Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Matriarch Cubs]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Matriarch Cubs ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +2%–20% chance to get a second [[Heavy Pearl]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HIGH_CLASS_ARCHFIEND_DICE
Tooltip: Straight from Kickback City!; Ability: Try Your Luck RIGHT CLICK Costs 6.7M coins to roll between 1-6.; Gain between -300 and +300 for 24h.; Overwritten by new rolls!; If you roll a 6, earn 100M coins but lose this dice.; Heavy in the hand. Could someone have tampered with it?; LEGENDARY
High Class Archfiend Dice is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
High Class Archfiend Dice is a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] (0.25%) drop obtained from the [[Inferno Demonlord]] Tier IV+ with [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer VII (7)]].
## Usage
Item:
High Class Archfiend Dice
[Effects]
[Ability: Try Your Luck RIGHT CLICK; Costs 6.7M coins to roll between 1-6.; Gain between -300❤ and +300❤ for 24h.; Overwritten by new rolls!; If you roll a 6, earn 100M coins but lose this dice.; Heavy in the hand. Could someone have tampered with it?]
To use a High Class Archfiend Dice, it costs 6,666,666 [[Coins]]. It will roll, and land on a number between 1 and 7. The player can gain between +300 [[Health]] and -300 [[Health]]. This effect lasts for 24 hours.
The dice first attempts to roll for a 7 (0.15% (1 in 666 rolls), can be buffed 2x–3x by [[Vincent]]). If this fails, it attempts to roll for a 6 (6.25% (1 in 16 rolls)). If this fails too, it rolls for a number between 1 and 5 with equal chances.
If the dice lands on a 6, the player gains 100,000,000 [[Coins]], but loses the dice. If the dice lands on a 7, the player gains an [[Archfiend Dye]], but loses the dice.
## 1x Archfiend Dye
|Number|Chance|Result|Loses Dice|
|-|-|-|-|
|1|18.72% (1 in 5.34 rolls)|-300 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|2|18.72% (1 in 5.34 rolls)|-200 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|3|18.72% (1 in 5.34 rolls)|-100 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|4|18.72% (1 in 5.34 rolls)|+100 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|5|18.72% (1 in 5.34 rolls)|+200 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|6|6.24% (1 in 16.02 rolls)|+300 [[Health]]; 100,000,000 [[Coins]]|✔ Yes|

## Tips
- Rolling during [[Vincent]]'s 2x-3x results in less profit due to the [[Archfiend Dye]] only selling for .
  - This also applies, to a lesser extent, to the [[Bucket of Dye]] and [[Attributes|Miracle Chance]].
## Notes
- The dice can be used even without [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer VII (7)]].
## Trivia
- The poll former admin 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon made on the 26th of February 2022 on gambling most likely helped inspire this item.
- Its description implies that the dice is rigged not in favor of the player.
- The dice have negative expected value.
- [[Sinful Dice]], [[Archfiend Dice]] and [[High Class Archfiend Dice]] High Class Archfiend Dice are one of few items in the game whose texture varies between items. Each has 6 texture variants, one for each side of the die.
  - Unlike all other items, [[Lucky Dice]] only has one. It is also the only Dice that got a new texture in the SkyBlock Resource Pack.
    - Oddly enough, the old sprite of [[Lucky Dice]], which had 5 pips on top is different than the sprite of 5 pips on top of any other Dice item.
    - Despite there being only 6 different sprites of Dice items, there are actually 48 possible variants of shown sides of a standard dice from an angle where 3 sides are shown.
  - When this item has been dropped on the ground and picked back up by a player, or when switched away from and back to in the hotbar, it will change its texture randomly to one of its six texture variants.

Tooltip: Health: +175; Defense: +55; Intelligence: +130; Mending: +5; Speed: +5; Gemstones: [⚔] [⚔]; Tiered Bonus: Spirit (0\ 4); Every 3s, up to 5 nearby player's attacks grant 1 stack of ⚶ Spirit. (Max 10 Stacks); Lose 1 after 4s of not gaining a stack.; Stacks can be used with a Hollow Wand to cast spells.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; ❣ Requires Kuudra Basic Tier Completion.; LEGENDARY HELMET
Hollow Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set. There are 4 upgraded tiers of Hollow Armor: Hot, Burning, Fiery, and Infernal, which can all be obtained by prestiging the previous tier.
## Obtaining
Basic Tier Hollow Armor Pieces can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Paid Reward Chests]] at the end of a [[Kuudra]] fight.
Piece Drop Chances:
- Basic Tier: 4.31%
- Hot Tier: 4.2%
- Burning Tier: 4.05%
- Fiery Tier: 3.91%
- Infernal Tier: 4.08%
Higher tiers of Hollow Armor can be obtained by prestiging the previous tier at a Blacksmith, which can be done if it has 10 [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] applied to it. After being prestiged, Armor pieces lose their Stars.

Up to 4 other players within 25 blocks can also grant you stacks by hitting mobs.; Stacks can be gained from each player at most once every 3s➜2s➜1s. The maximum number of stacks is determined by the lowest prestige tier of the equipped Hollow Armor pieces.; Each Spirit ⚶ stack can be used as charge for the Hollow Wand.; Pieces Required: 2+]
Upon having at least 2 pieces equipped, the Tiered Set Bonus: Spirit is unlocked. When the wearer of the armor or up to 4 other players within 25 blocks hits an enemy, the wearer gains 1 stack, with a cooldown dependent on how many pieces are worn - 3s if wearing 2 pieces, 2s if wearing 3, and 1s if wearing all 4. This cooldown is player-dependent and not armor-dependent, meaning if 3 pieces of Hollow Armor are worn, up to 5 charges can be gained every 2 seconds if the wearer and 4 other players all attack enemies. The maximum number of stacks is determined by the lowest prestige tier among the equipped Hollow Armor pieces. Magic damage can also grant stacks. These stacks do not do anything by themselves but are used to cast spells from the [[Hollow Wand]].

## Trivia
- This set's appearance seems to be inspired by Terraria's Hallowed Armor Set.
- It's been observed by some community members that the usage of the word "Stacks" and how they function could be a potential reference to the Risk of Rain series.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- A total of 141,005 (2,203 stacks plus 13) [[Crimson Essence]], 45 [[Heavy Pearl]], 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Kuudra Teeth]], and 44,740,000 [[Coins]] are required for one Infernal tier piece, costing .
  - A total of 1,793,505 (28,023 stacks plus 33) [[Crimson Essence]], 97 (1 stack plus 33) [[Heavy Pearl]], 460 (7 stacks plus 12) [[Kuudra Teeth]], and 139,175,000 [[Coins]] are needed from scratch for a single Infernal piece with 15 [[Star Upgrades|Stars]], costing .

# Hollow Wand
Categories: Legendary items, Weapons, Wands, Kuudra
[Hollow Wand]
Type: Wand ([[Wands]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Combat Level ([[Combat]]) Requirement: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]]); [[Kuudra]] Basic Tier Completion
Stats: [[Damage]]: 150; [[Strength]]: 20; [[Intelligence]]: 120
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Sell: 10,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HOLLOW_WAND
Tooltip: Damage: +150; Strength: +20; Intelligence: +120; Ability: Hollow Spirit LEFT\ RIGHT CLICK Left Click to cast a stack of [✤] and Right Click to cast a stack of [✦]. Combine stacks to cast different spells!; ⊳ [✤✤] Spirit Spark (10 ⚶); ⊳ [✦✦] Hollowed Rush (15 ⚶); ⊳ [✦✤] Raging Wind (20 ⚶); ⊳ [✤✦] Ichor Pool (30 ⚶); Type '\ wandinfo' for detailed ability info.; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; ❣ Requires Kuudra Basic Tier Completion.; LEGENDARY WAND
The Hollow Wand is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Wands|Wand]]. It requires [[Combat|Combat XXII (22)]] and [[Kuudra]] Basic Tier Completion to use.
## Obtaining
The Hollow Wand can sometimes appear in Paid [[Kuudra|Reward Chests]] in [[Kuudra]] fights.
Drop Chances:
- Basic Tier: 3.45%
- Hot Tier: 3.36%
- Burning Tier: 3.24%
- Fiery Tier: 3.24%
## Usage
Wand:
Hollow Wand [[Damage]]: +150 [[Strength]]: +20 [[Intelligence]]: +120
[Effects]
[Ability: Hollow Spirit LEFT/RIGHT CLICK; Left Click to cast a stack of [✤] and Right Click to cast a stack of [✦]. Combine stacks to cast different spells!; ⊳ [✤✤] Spirit Spark (10 ⚶); ⊳ [✦✦] Hollowed Rush (15 ⚶); ⊳ [✦✤] Raging Wind (20 ⚶); ⊳ [✤✦] Ichor Pool (30 ⚶); Type '/wandinfo' for detailed ability info.; Pieces Required: 2+]
At least 2 pieces of [[Hollow Armor]] must be worn to get Spirit ⚶ stacks required for Spells. The effects of the Spells do not stack when used by multiple players unless otherwise specified.
Typing /wandinfo in chat while holding the Hollow Wand shows every Spell the Hollow Wand can use:
- Spell: Spirit Spark [✤✤] (10 ⚶) Heal yourself and up to 5 players within 25 blocks for 8%❤ max HP.
- Spell: Hollowed Rush [✦✦] (15 ⚶) Grant up to 5 players within 25 blocks +1.3x [[Mana Regen]] for 6s.
- Spell: Raging Wind [✦✤] (20 ⚶) Grant up to 5 players within 25 blocks +30% [[Damage]] and +10 [[Ferocity]] for 20s. Stacks up to 3 times.
- Spell: Ichor Pool [✤✦] (30 ⚶) Spawn a pool with 8 block radius. Up to 5 players standing within this pool gains +1.2x [[Damage]] and +40 [[Ferocity]]. Pool lasts for 20s.
Due to the stack requirements of the abilities, certain abilities require higher tiers of [[Hollow Armor]] to use:
- Hollowed Rush: Hot or above
- Raging Wind: Burning or above
- Ichor Pool: Fiery or above

Holly Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
A Holly Dye has a base 0.0125% (1 in 8,000) chance of appearing in a [[Red Gift]]. Both the gifting player and recipient receive the item.
## Usage
Holly Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece that has base texture of [[Leather Armor]] by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #3C6746.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.
## Trivia
- When checking for [[Bucket of Dye]] and [[Attributes|Miracle Chance]], only the user opening the [[Red Gift]] applies.

# Hologram
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Confirmations needed, Bits Shop, Furniture
[Hologram]
Type: Furniture Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 2,000 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HOLOGRAM
Tooltip: Furniture Colored; Add some flair to your island with custom floating text!; LEGENDARY
The Hologram is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Furniture]] item that can be used to display text on the player's [[Private Island]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Hologram can be purchased from [[Elizabeth]] in the [[Community Center]] for 2,000 [[Bits]].
## Usage
The Hologram is intended to be placed on the ground, and has only a small model that generates particles. The color of those particles can be changed by the player to any of Minecraft's default 16 colors, or disabled entirely. The model itself can also be disabled.
The Hologram projects text, in the style of a mob's name tag. Up to three lines of text can be written. Text input is though the Minecraft chat box. When editing Hologram text, ensure the message begins with "/hgl", or the Hologram's text will not update.
### Formatting Modifiers
Using the '&' symbol, followed by a number between '0' and '9', or a letter between 'a' and 'f' will change the color of the text immediately after the identifier.
You can also use the '&' symbol to add several kinds of formatting: &l Bold, &o Italic, &n Underline, &m Strikethrough[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], and &k Obfuscated (example).
### Tips
- The Hologram's model cannot be disabled unless at least one line of text is written.
- A single line of text may contain no more than 35 characters.
  - The 35 character limit includes any characters used for color modifiers.
## Gallery
- A render of the hologram base.
- A Hologram placed with default options and "Hello," "SkyBlock", and "World" input as text.
- The possible colors available to Holograms.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added Hologram.|

Holy Dragon Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set that is focused on [[Healing]]. The full set can be crafted from 240 (3 stacks plus 48) [[Dragon Fragment|Holy Dragon Fragments]]. Holy Dragon Armor gives the most health of all 8 different [[Dragon Armor]] sets.
[[Combat|Combat XVI (16)]] is required to be able to use this set and, if a dungeon item, [[Catacombs|Catacombs X (10)]] to be able to use this set while in [[Dungeons]].

## Tips
- While ideal for survival, this armor set should not be relied on too heavily. The [[Health Regen]] will fall short for careless players in high level encounters, even when playing [[Healer]].
  - It has a niche use in the [[Trials of Fire]] where it can help push another player's healing above the threshold for the next trial.
## Trivia
- Unlike other [[Dragon Fragment|Dragon Fragments]], Holy Dragon Fragments can only be obtained by killing [[Lost Adventurer|Lost Adventurers]] that are wearing Holy Dragon Armor due to the inability to spawn a Holy Dragon in the End.
- For a few days after the Dungeons Update released, Holy Fragments couldn't be sold to the Bazaar, but instead had to be auctioned. A few days later, however, they were moved to the Bazaar along with the [[Recombobulator 3000]], and [[Fuming Potato Book]].
- The Set Bonus is bugged and doesn't work on the wearer, but it works on other players.
- The [[Catacombs|Catacombs XXVII (27)]] requirement is likely a bug, as the item was stated to have been changed to a [[Catacombs|Catacombs XV (15)]] requirement in patch notes. However, this change never occurred. This bug was patched as of Dec 13, 2022. As of September 2023, the requirement was lowered to Catacombs 10.
- This armor is one of the four dragon armors that have coloring similar to that of the main "stat" bonus. Unlike the other three armors that have similar colors to their stats, those being [[Strong Dragon Armor]], [[Young Dragon Armor]], and [[Wise Dragon Armor]], Holy Dragon Armor focuses on a more broad term, being healing, generally associated with the color green. Arguably, Holy Dragon Armor could be focused on the [[Mending]] stat.

Hypergolic Gabagool is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that is an upgraded form of [[Heavy Gabagool]]. It can be used as a [[Pet Candies|Pet Food]] on Gabagool [[Pets]] giving 3,276,800 base experience. It can also be used to create special fuel for [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minions]]. Unlike the previous tiers, minion fuel made with this tier will sometimes produce special items.
## Obtaining

|-|-|-|

|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]; ( [[Blaze Rod]])|None|[[Hypergolic Gabagool]] 1x Hypergolic Gabagool 2x [[Inferno Fuel Block]] 6x Blaze Rod Distillate ([[Distillates]])|Recipe: [[Distillates]] ×6 + [[Inferno Fuel Block]] ×2 + Hypergolic Gabagool → [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]|

### Inferno Minion Fuel
[[Inferno Minion Fuel]] created with Hypergolic Gabagool produces unique items when placed in an [[Inferno Minion]]. These are:
- [[Chili Pepper]]
- [[Inferno Apex]]
- [[Inferno Vertex]]
- [[Gabagool the Fish]]
- [[Reaper Pepper]]
### Gabagool Pets
Hypergolic Gabagool can be fed to Gabagool [[Pets]] to give them 3,276,800 Pet exp.

Tooltip: Gear Score: 615 (674); Damage: +260 (+286); Strength: +150 (+165); Ferocity: +30 (+30); Intelligence: +350 (+385); Gemstones: [] [⚔]; Deals +50% damage to  Wither mobs.; Grants +1  Damage and +2  Intelligence per Catacombs level.; Right-click to use your class ability!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor VII Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON SWORD
The Hyperion is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked upon completion of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
The Hyperion is one of the four [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] variants, alongside the [[Valkyrie]], [[Astraea]], and [[Scylla]], focusing on [[Intelligence]] and [[Mage]] builds.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|8x [[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] [[Astraea]] [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: [[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Scylla]]/[[Astraea]]/[[Valkyrie]] → Hyperion|
## Usage
Sword:
Hyperion [[Damage]]: +260 [[Strength]]: +150 [[Ferocity]]: +30 [[Intelligence]]: +350
[Effects]
[Deals +50% damage to Wither mobs.; Grants +1 Damage and +2 Intelligence per Catacombs level.; Right-click to use your class ability!]
### Crafting
The Hyperion can be crafted into another [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] variant using the corresponding recipe, keeping all enchantments and upgrades. Items used in the crafting recipe are consumed.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Valkyrie]]|None|8 [[Diamante's Handle]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] [[Hyperion]] Hyperion [[Astraea]]|Recipe: [[Diamante's Handle]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Scylla]]/Hyperion/[[Astraea]] → [[Valkyrie]]|
|[[Astraea]]|None|8 [[Jolly Pink Rock]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] [[Hyperion]] Hyperion [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: [[Jolly Pink Rock]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Scylla]]/Hyperion/[[Valkyrie]] → [[Astraea]]|
|[[Scylla]]|None|8 [[Bigfoot's Bola]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Hyperion]] Hyperion [[Astraea]] [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: [[Bigfoot's Bola]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/Hyperion/[[Astraea]]/[[Valkyrie]] → [[Scylla]]|
It can have [[Necron's Blade Scrolls]] applied to it, granting their effects on right click.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|150|150|
|✪✪|300|450|
|✪✪✪|500|950|
|✪✪✪✪|900; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,850; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|1,500; 25,000 [[Coins]]|3,350; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- The [[Reforging|Heroic]] reforge pairs well with the Hyperion, as does [[Reforging|Fabled]] or [[Reforging|Withered]] for Left-Click Mage depending on the player's stats.
- It is best to donate a Hyperion to the [[Museum]] to grant credit for all 5 variants.
## Trivia
- The Hyperion references the Greek Titan Hyperion, the Titan of light and observation in Greek mythology and certain adaptations and translations of Roman mythology.
- Donating the Hyperion to the [[Museum]] grants credit for all [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] variants.

# Iceberg Dye
Aliases: Iceberg Dye (Old)
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Mob Drops, Cosmetics
[Iceberg Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_ICEBERG
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #71A6D2!; LEGENDARY DYE
Iceberg Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
The Iceberg Dye can be obtained by killing [[Sea Creatures|Winter Sea Creatures]]. The drop chance of this [[Dyes|Dye]] may be temporarily doubled or tripled by [[Vincent]].
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv3]|Frosty ([[Frosty (mob)]])|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/2.5M|
|[Lv1]|[[Frozen Steve]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/2.5M|
|[Lv25]|[[Grinch]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/500k|
|[Lv115]|[[Nutcracker]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/500k|
|[Lv186]|[[Reindrake]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/50k|
|[Lv175]|[[Yeti]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/50k|
## Usage
Iceberg Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #71A6D2. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.
## Trivia
- Before SkyBlock 0.20.5, the Iceberg Dye was the only [[Dyes|Dye]] to use a block's texture instead of that of a dye.

- Iceberg Bucket of Dye Iceberg [[Bucket of Dye]]

The Icicle Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Icicle Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on October 10, 2020. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 1,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Icicle Skin can be applied to a [[Frozen Blaze Armor|Frozen Blaze Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance.

The Inferno Apex is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] material that can be crafted or rarely generated by an [[Inferno Minion]].
## Obtaining
### Inferno Minions
The Inferno Apex has a 0.00007639% (1/1,309,091) chance to be created by an [[Inferno Minion]] using Hypergolic [[Inferno Minion Fuel|Inferno Minion Fuels]] for every item harvested. [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minion X]] and higher tiers generate an extra Inferno Apex every time one is created due to its Apex Minion ability.
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|16x [[Hypergolic Ionized Ceramics]] 64x [[Molten Powder]] 64x [[Inferno Vertex]]|Recipe: [[Inferno Vertex]] x16 ×4 + [[Molten Powder]] x16 ×4 + [[Hypergolic Ionized Ceramics]] x16 → Inferno Apex|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Inferno Minion XI ([[Inferno Minion]])|Blaze Slayer III (3) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|[[Inferno Apex]] 1x Inferno Apex 1x Inferno Minion X ([[Inferno Minion]]) 1x Inferno Minion VIII ([[Inferno Minion]]) 48x [[Inferno Vertex]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] x64 ×4 + Inferno Apex + [[Inferno Vertex]] x24 ×2 + [[Inferno Minion]] ×2 → [[Inferno Minion]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|Blaze Slayer VII (7) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Subzero Inverter]] [[Inferno Apex]] 1x Inferno Apex 2x [[Bluetooth Ring]] 16x [[Inferno Vertex]] 64x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] x64 ×2 + Inferno Apex + [[Bluetooth Ring]] ×2 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + [[Subzero Inverter]] + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x64 → [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|
|[[SOS Flare]]|Blaze Slayer VII (7) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Wilson's Engineering Plans]] [[Inferno Apex]] 1x Inferno Apex 1x [[Alert Flare]] 16x [[Inferno Vertex]] 32x [[Enchanted Sulphur]] 128x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + [[Enchanted Sulphur]] x32 + Inferno Apex + [[Molten Powder]] x64 ×2 + [[Wilson's Engineering Plans]] + [[Alert Flare]] → [[SOS Flare]]|
## Trivia
- All items that require Inferno Apexes to craft also require [[Molten Powder]] and [[Inferno Vertex|Inferno Vertices]] to craft.

The InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Vacuums|Vacuum]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
|Merchant|Price|
|-|-|
|SkyMart ([[The Garden]])|2,500 [[Copper]] 1x [[Chirping Stereo]] 1x [[InfiniVacuum™]]|
## Usage
Vacuum:
InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius [[Damage]]: +400 [[Farming Fortune]]: +25
[Effects]
[Ability: Vacuum HOLD RIGHT CLICK; Deals 400 Damage per second to nearby Pests (Pest (Mob Type)). Ability: Pest Tracker LEFT CLICK; Leads you in the direction of the nearest Pest (Pest (Mob Type)).; Cooldown: 1s Ability: Stereo Harmony SNEAK LEFT CLICK; Plays vinyls at a frequency only Pests (Pest (Mob Type)) can hear. Certain Pests are more likely to appear when certain vinyls are playing.]
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
The InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius can be used to capture [[Pests]] from up to 15 blocks away. When used near a pest, it will deal 400 [[Damage]] per second to the [[Pests|Pest]]. [[Pests (Currency)|Pests]] captured with the InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius will be sucked up, and can be exchanged at [[Pesthunter Phillip]] for temporary [[Farming Fortune]] and various goods at his shop.
Left clicking with the InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius will create a trail of particles leading to the nearest Pest.
By using Sneak left click you open a GUI allowing you to place vinyls, that offer a higher chance for a certain pest to spawn depending on each vinyl.
Stereo Harmony works even when offline as long as a vinyl is equipped.
## Trivia
- Applying the [[Reforging|Buzzing]] reforge and 5 [[Bookworm's Favorite Book]] to the InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius will make it deal 900 [[Damage]], which is more than the [[Health]] of [[Pests]], allowing the player to one-tap them, also bypassing the [[Earthworm]]'s burrowing mechanism.
  - While [[Derpy]] is mayor, Pests have 1,200 [[Health]], therefore [[Earthworm|Earthworms]] will still burrow with a maxed out vacuum unless using the [[Hedgehog Pet]], which doubles damage dealt to pests at level [Lv100].
    - [Lv100] [[Hedgehog Pet]] also allows for [[Pests]] to be one-shot without using [[Reforging|Buzzing]], unless [[Derpy]] is mayor. This allows players to use [[Reforging|Beady]] for extra [[Farming Fortune]] against Pests.
- There is a bug that causes vinyls to randomly disappear from the stereo sometimes. It is believed this may happen when adding multiple vinyls in quick succession.
- The name of this item is probably a reference to the popular vacuum cleaner brand Hoover.
- The Stereo Harmony ability is likely named in reference to Forever Bound's song Stereo Madness, Widely known as the song of the first level in Geometry Dash.

Iridium is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item used to buy the [[AquaMaster™ Hydromax]].
## Obtaining
Iridium has a 0.2% (1 in 500) chance to drop from a [[Greenhouse|Harvest Bounty]] in the [[Greenhouse]].
## Usage
Iridium can be traded with 2,500 [[Copper]] and 1 [[AquaMaster™ X]] at [[The Garden|SkyMart]] to obtain an [[AquaMaster™ Hydromax]].

The Jacobus Register is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] obtainable by buying upgrades of [[Jacobus]].
## Obtaining
The Jacobus Register is obtained by buying 10 Accessory Bag slots from [[Jacobus]], which cost a total of 21,500,000 [[Coins]].
Once obtained, it can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] for 2,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Trivia
- It is currently the only accessory that gives [[Pet Luck]].
- It is automatically [[Soulbound]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Tooltip: Foraging Pet; Perks:; ???; Hatches at level 100; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; LEGENDARY
The Jade Dragon Pet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] pet purchased in the [[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)|Dragon's Lair]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Obtaining
A Jade Dragon Egg can be purchased from the dragon residing in the [[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)|Dragon's Lair]] below the [[Murkwater Depths]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]]. A Jade Dragon Egg can be purchased by clicking one of the eggs. The Jade Dragon Egg costs:
- 500,000,000 [[Coins]]
- 1x [[Shiniest]]
- 64x [[Figstone]]
- 64x [[Mangcore]]
- 64x [[Helixis]]
## Usage
Both the Egg and the Pet have the type of [[Foraging]] Pet. From level 1 to level 99, the Jade Dragon Egg has no stats nor abilities, and cannot hold [[Pet Items]].
Upon reaching level 100, it will hatch into a Jade Dragon Pet and gain its stats and perks.
## Stats
[[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Jade Dragon Pet
|Starting Stats (Level 101) ⓘ (Stats of a pet at starting level)|Max Stats (Level 200) ⓘ (Stats of a maxed-out pet)VED|
|-|-|
|Stats: [[Strength]]: +25.25 [[Magic Find]]: +5.05 [[Foraging Fortune]]: +25.25 Abilities: Forest Power – Adds +75.25 [[Strength]] and +37.63 (Rounded off to 2 decimal places; The exact value is 37.625.) [[Speed]] to all your Axes. Jade Scale – Grants +15 [[Foraging Fortune]] and +4 [[Sweep]] for every digit in your Mangrove Collection. (Max 10M collection) Dragon's Pride – Grants +1 [[Foraging Fortune]] per 5 [[Sweep]]. Apex Predator – Increases your total [[Sweep]] by 0.1% for every Maxed out Attribute you unlocked. Symbiosis – If you own a level 200 Jade Dragon, Grants +4 [[Foraging Fortune]] for every other unique maxed Foraging Pet that you own.|Stats: [[Strength]]: +50 [[Magic Find]]: +10 [[Foraging Fortune]]: +50 Abilities: Forest Power – Adds +100 [[Strength]] and +50 [[Speed]] to all your Axes. Jade Scale – Grants +15 [[Foraging Fortune]] and +4 [[Sweep]] for every digit in your Mangrove Collection. (Max 10M collection) Dragon's Pride – Grants +1 [[Foraging Fortune]] per 5 [[Sweep]]. Apex Predator – Increases your total [[Sweep]] by 0.1% for every Maxed out Attribute you unlocked. Symbiosis – If you own a level 200 Jade Dragon, Grants +4 [[Foraging Fortune]] for every other unique maxed Foraging Pet that you own.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_JADE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #00A86B!; LEGENDARY DYE
Jade Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Jade Dye has a 0.0002% (1 in 500,000) chance to be obtained from a [[Crystal Nucleus]] loot bundle.
## Usage
Jade Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #00A86B. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.
## Gallery
- Jade Dyed Leather Armor
- Jade Bucket of Dye Jade [[Bucket of Dye]]

# Jerry Barn Skin

[Jerry Barn Skin]
Type: Barn Skin ([[Barn Skins]])

Item ID: JERRY_BARN_SKIN
Tooltip: Consume this item to unlock the Jerry Barn Skin on The Garden!; Click to consume!; LEGENJERRY COSMETIC
The Jerry Barn Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENJERRY]] [[Barn Skins|Barn Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Jerry Barn Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on March 31, 2023. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 2,000 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 8,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Jerry Barn Skin can be consumed on the [[The Garden|Garden]] and then used at [[The Garden|The Desk]] to change the look of the center plot.
## Trivia
- The Jerry Barn Skin is the only item in the game to be given a unique rarity, [[Rarity|LEGENJERRY]]. However it works the same as [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] items.
- If [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]], its tooltip will say a LEGENJERRY COSMETIC a.

The Jerry Castle Greenhouse Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Greenhouse Skins|Greenhouse Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Jerry Castle Greenhouse Skin can be purchased from the Spring 2026 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it costs 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Jerry Castle Greenhouse Skin can be consumed on the [[The Garden|Garden]] and then used at [[The Garden|The Desk]] to change the look of a [[Greenhouse]] plot.
## History

|Mar 21, 2026|Added Jerry Castle Greenhouse Skin.|

# Jerryflower
Aliases: Jerry Flower, Jerry flower
Categories: Legendary items, Items, More info needed, Mutations
[Jerryflower]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Fertilized Jerryseed]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JERRYFLOWER
Tooltip: Jerryflower never sleeps, Jerryflower never stops, Jerryflower is always watching.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; * Co-op Soulbound * LEGENDARY MUTATION
A Jerryflower is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A [[Fertilized Jerryseed]] can be planted on farmland in the [[Greenhouse]] to grow a Jerryflower.
While growing, a Jerryflower does not need water. It also does not provide any bonuses to nearby crops when placed.
At Growth Stage 5, the [[Fertilized Jerryseed]] stops growing until given 10 [[Move Jerry]].
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Jerryflowers drop no crops and [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
A Jerryflower can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 20,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 10 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Jerryflower grants an additional 250 [[Copper]].
## Trivia
- Jerryflower and [[Godseed]] are the only [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mutations]] not required for [[Rose Dragon Pet]].
- Unlike all other mutations, Jerryflower doesn't appear in [[Carpenter]]'s and [[Jake]]'s "All mutations" menu until discovered.
  - Jerryflower is also the only Mutation that cannot be placed in the [[Mutations Sack|Mutations Sacks]].
## Gallery
- Jerryflower in the Greenhouse Jerryflower in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History

|Dec 5, 2025|Added Jerryflower.|
|Dec 10, 2025|Made Jerryflower Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Jerryflower.|

The Judgement Core is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item used to craft the [[Terminator]]. It is a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drop from the [[Voidgloom Seraph]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
The Judgement Core has a 0.06% (1/1,771.12) chance to drop from Tier IV [[Voidgloom Seraph]] if the player has unlocked [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL VII (7)]].
### Raffle of the Century
Judgement Core can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Terminator]]|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|[[Judgement Core]] 1x Judgement Core 3x [[Null Blade]] 4x [[Braided Griffin Feather]] 8x [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Tarantula Silk]]|Recipe: [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] x4 ×2 + [[Null Blade]] ×3 + [[Tarantula Silk]] x64 ×2 + Judgement Core + [[Braided Griffin Feather]] x4 → [[Terminator]]|
## Trivia
- The Judgement Core resembles the Anger Core from Portal, though more visually resembles a Core from Portal 2.
- The Judgement Core references Terminator 2: Judgement Day as it is the primary component in crafting the [[Terminator]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 31, 2021|0.11.5|Added Judgement Core.|
|Jun 4, 2021|Judgement Core is now used in the [[Terminator]] recipe.|

Jungle Heart is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
The Jungle Heart can be obtained from Loot Chests and Treasure Chests in the [[Jungle]] area within the [[Crystal Hollows]]. It has a 1/107.19 chance per roll to appear in high-tier chests (which have a 5% chance to replace a low-tier chest), making the final drop rate 1/476.4 per chest.
It has an increased chance of being found in chests at the end of the [[Jungle Temple]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Usage
Jungle Hearts can be traded with [[Odawa]]. You can buy a [[Tribal Spear]] using [[Jungle Heart]] 1 Jungle Heart and 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Sludge Juice]], or a [[Jungle Amulet]] with [[Jungle Heart]] 4 Jungle Heart and 500 (7 stacks plus 52) [[Sludge Juice]].
## Trivia
- The texture used to be the same as the [[Armor of the Rising Sun|Helmet of the Rising Sun]] before it was changed in a later update.

# Kuudra Follower Relic
Categories: Legendary items, Accessories
[Kuudra Follower Relic]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])

[Upgrades]

[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KUUDRA_FOLLOWER_RELIC
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +16; Reduces damage taken from Kuudra Followers by 65%.; * Co-op Soulbound * LEGENDARY ACCESSORY
The Kuudra Follower Relic is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that reduces damage taken from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Followers]] by 65%.
## Obtaining
The Kuudra Follower Relic can be obtained from [[Crag]] at the [[Forgotten Skull]] on the [[Crimson Isle]]. The player must be wearing full [[Kuudra Follower Armor]], and have the [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] and [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]] in their inventory. After clicking on the Kuudra Gatekeeper with the conditions met and confirming, the Kuudra Follower Artifact will be upgraded into the Kuudra Follower Relic.
## Usage
Accessory:
Kuudra Follower Relic
[Effects]
[Reduces damage taken from Kuudra Followers (Kuudra Follower) by 65%.]
## Bugs
- Although the condition says "have the [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] and [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]] in their inventory", the quest can proceed even if the Artifact is in the [[Accessory Bag]].
  - In this case, the [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] will fail to be upgraded to this tier.
  - Right after the release of the Relic, there wasn't another chance to upgrade to it if the bug happened. At some point between April 20 and 23 in 2023, a player can trigger the event again with completed ([[Rarity|SPECIAL]]) [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]] without the need of wearing [[Kuudra Follower Armor]] again.
## Trivia
- The Kuudra Follower Relic grants the best source of damage reduction against any one kind of mob, this being mobs in [[Kuudra]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added the Kuudra Follower Relic.|
|Unknown Date|The event of upgrading the Artifact ([[Kuudra Follower Artifact]]) to the [[Kuudra Follower Relic]] Relic can now be triggered again if the bug prevented the upgrading before.|
|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Changed from Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) to [[Soulbound]].|

# Kuudra Mandible
Aliases: Chomp, Kuudra's Mandible, Kuudra's mandible, Mandible, Womandible

Applied To: [[Fishing Rods]]

[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KUUDRA_MANDIBLE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Chomp reforge when combined with a Fishing Rod.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Crit Chance  Fishing Speed; Chomp Bonus Each unique Lava Sea Creature killed with this rod in your inventory reduces their  Health when fished by 1%.; Capped at -20% health.; Current Reduction: -0% ❤; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 33.; LEGENDARY REFORGE STONE
The Kuudra Mandible is an [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Chomp]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], which grants [[Strength]], [[Crit Chance]] and [[Fishing Speed]].
## Obtaining
The Kuudra Mandible is a rare drop from Burning [[Kuudra]] and above, once the player has obtained [[Kuudra Follower Relic]] and maxed out the [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Kuudra Mandible can be applied to [[Fishing Rods]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Chomp]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], [[Crit Chance]] and [[Fishing Speed]], which scale with the Fishing Rod's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXXIII (33)]]. A Fishing Rod that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Chomp]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Chomp Bonus, which reduces the [[Health]] of caught [[Sea Creatures|Lava Sea Creatures]] by 1%, for every unique [[Sea Creatures|Lava Sea Creature]] killed while the Fishing Rod was in the player's inventory, up to 20%.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|

## Tips
- [[Reforging|Chomp]] is the best reforge for overall [[Fishing Speed]], and is best-in-slot for [[Trophy Fish|Trophy Fishing]].
  - [[Reforging|Pitchin']] is both cheaper and better for general fishing due to granting [[Sea Creature Chance]], only being worst by 1 [[Fishing Speed]].
## Notes
- The maximum reduction of the special effect is 20%.
  - The cap was reached on October 15th, 2026[Confirm (Confirmation needed; no set date?; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] when the [[Plhlegblast]] started counting as a unique [[Sea Creatures|Lava Sea Creature]]. Previously the maximum reduction was 19%.
  - Despite not being under Lava Sea Creature group in Bestiary and spawning even without fishing, [[Stridersurfer]] does count for the effect.
- The Sea Creatures don't need to be killed with the rod itself to count toward the special effect.
- Having multiple [[Reforging|Chomp]] Fishing Rods when killing a unique Lava Sea Creature will only progress the bonus on one of the rods.
- The Sea Creature [[Health]] reduction stacks multiplicatively with [[Frail]].
## Trivia
- [[Reforging|Chomp]] is tied with [[Reforging|Hasty]] to be the best reforge for [[Crit Chance]].

## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Lapis Creeper Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Lapis Skeleton Shard]]
- Second: [[Ghost Shard]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or higher

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Lapis Creeper Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Book Wisdom]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Book Wisdom ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Enchanting Wisdom]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Tooltip: Gear Score: 460 (502); Damage: +200 (+220); Strength: +180 (+198); Crit Damage: +50% (+55%); Reduces the defense of your target by 10% of their max  Defense on hit, stacking up to 5 times.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor V Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON BOW
The Last Breath is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Bows|Bow]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
A Last Breath can be claimed once as a reward from [[Livid|Livid V (5)]], which is obtained after killing [[Livid]] 500 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Last Breath can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor V]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]])|Bedrock|7,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.2632% (1 in 380 runs)|0.4004% (1 in 249.7 runs)|350|1|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|7,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.3086% (1 in 324 runs)|0.4704% (1 in 212.6 runs)|350|1|
## Usage
Bow:
Last Breath [[Damage]]: +200 [[Strength]]: +180 [[Crit Damage]]: +50
[Effects]
[Reduces the Defense of your target by 10% of their max Defense, stacking up to 5 times.]
Bow:
[[Last Breath]] [[Damage]]: +210 [[Strength]]: +190 [[Crit Damage]]: +50
[Effects]
[Reduces the Defense of your target by 10% of their max Defense, stacking up to 5 times.]
The Last Breath, being a bow, can be used for ranged damage: it has a unique property that allows the wielder to reduce the max [[Defense]] of their target by 10%, stacking up to 5 times.
## Upgrading
A Last Breath can be upgraded into Last Breath using 8 [[Livid Fragment|Livid Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +10 [[Damage]]
- +10 [[Strength]]
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Last Breath]] 1x Last Breath 8x [[Livid Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Livid Fragment]] ×8 + Last Breath → [[Last Breath]]|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|20|20|
|✪✪|50|70|
|✪✪✪|100|170|
|✪✪✪✪|200; 10,000 [[Coins]]|370; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|300; 25,000 [[Coins]]|670; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- While the Last Breath may fall short as a primary weapon compared to others of its rarity, it has strategic use in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Master Mode - Floor VII]] as it can be used to reduce the [[Defense]] of the Withered Dragons that spawn during [[The Wither King]]'s phase. You can use [[Twilight Arrow Poison]] in combination with the Last Breath for extra damage.
## Trivia
- The Last Breath is a reference to Yasuo's ultimate, Last Breath, from League of Legends.

The Livid Dagger is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
A Livid Dagger can be claimed once as a reward from [[Livid|Livid III (3)]], which is obtained after killing [[Livid]] 150 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Livid Dagger can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in the [[The Catacombs - Floor V]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]])|Obsidian|7,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.2959% (1 in 338 runs)|0.2959% (1 in 338 runs)|320|1|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]])|Bedrock|7,000,000 [[Coins]]|1.0526% (1 in 95 runs)|1.7223% (1 in 58.1 runs)|320|4|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|7,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.2933% (1 in 341 runs)|0.495% (1 in 202 runs)|320|1|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|7,000,000 [[Coins]]|1.7656% (1 in 56.6 runs)|2.3356% (1 in 42.8 runs)|320|4|
## Usage
Sword:
Livid Dagger [[Damage]]: +210 [[Strength]]: +60 [[Crit Chance]]: +100 [[Crit Damage]]: +50 [[Attack Speed]]: +50
[Effects]
[Your Critical Hits deal 100% more damage if you are behind your target. Ability: Throw RIGHT CLICK; Throw your dagger at your enemies!; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 150; Cooldown: 5s]
The Livid Dagger, being a sword, can be used as a melee weapon. It has the unique property of increasing the damage of the wielder's critical hits by +100% if they are behind their target.
Its Throw ability allows the wielder to throw their sword forward, dealing damage to enemies in its path.
## Essence Crafting

|✪✪|60|90|

|✪✪✪✪|250; 10,000 [[Coins]]|460; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|400; 25,000 [[Coins]]|860; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- Applying the [[Reforging|Fast]] reforge to a Livid Dagger allows it to reach +100% [[Attack Speed]].
## Trivia
- Surprisingly, the Livid Dagger cannot be upgraded with [[Livid Fragment|Livid Fragments]].

Livid Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|

Livid Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece that has base texture of [[Leather Armor]] by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #CEB7AA.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.
