The Lord Jawbus Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Crimson Hook]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Lord Jawbus Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Lord Jawbus]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Kuudra
A Lord Jawbus Shard can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Loot]].
### Fusion
A Lord Jawbus Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Lord Jawbus Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Crimson Hook]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Crimson Hook ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Double Hook Chance]] while on Combat Islands.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Lord Jawbus Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Lord Jawbus Shard has the following description:
- The embodiment of raw power, his massive jaws are capable of crushing through the hardest of shells and stone.
## Trivia
- Despite being a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Lord Jawbus Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LOTUS_CROWN
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Trophies are +10% more likely to be DIAMOND.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; A splendid, yet tiny crown; just about the right size for your pet!; LEGENDARY PET ITEM
A Lotus Crown is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that increases the chance for fished [[Trophy Frogs]] and [[Trophy Fish]] to be DIAMOND.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Prince's Crown Jewel]] 1x [[Lotus Water Orb]] 2x [[Condensed Lily Pad]] 5x [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|Recipe: [[Condensed Lily Pad]] ×2 + [[Prince's Crown Jewel]] + [[Enchanted Gold Block]] ×5 + [[Lotus Water Orb]] → Lotus Crown|
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Lotus Crown will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +10% chance for [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] to be more likely to be DIAMOND. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Lotus Crown for 500,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.

Shard Item ID: MACAW
Tooltip: Splendor I Grants a +2.5% chance to get a bonus Rainbow Feather when hunting a SPARKLING  Critter in the Critter Safari.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 20.; LEGENDARY FOREST SHARD (ID L22)
The Macaw Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Splendor]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Macaw Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Macaw]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Macaw Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Splendor]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Splendor ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +2.5%–25% chance to get a bonus [[Rainbow Feather]] when hunting SPARKLING Critter ([[Critters]]) in the [[Critter Safari]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Macaw Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Macaw Shard has the following description:
- Macaws mate for life, with pairs of them quickly becoming inseparable and accompanying each other on their travels.

The Magic Top Hat is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item used to upgrade the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Snowman Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
## Obtaining
The Magic Top Hat can be purchased from [[Einary]] for 500 [[North Stars]] and 10 [[Volcanic Rock]].
## Usage
The Magic Top Hat can be given to [[Kat]] alongside with 1,000,000 [[Coins]] at [Lv1] to upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Snowman Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
The [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Snowman Pet]] unlocks the following perk:
|Perk|[Lv1]|[Lv100]|
|-|-|-|
|Ouch!|Your snowballs have 50% chance of dealing double damage!|Your snowballs have 50% chance of dealing double damage!|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MAGMA_BUCKET
Tooltip: Minion Fuel; Increases the speed of your minion by 30%. Unlimited Duration!; LEGENDARY
Magma Bucket is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Minion Fuel]] that increases [[Minions|Minion]] speed by 30%.
It has an unlimited duration and can be freely taken from minions at any given moment, without being destroyed, as its effect is permanent.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Heat Core]] 2x [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]] ×2 + [[Heat Core]] → Magma Bucket|
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
Magma Buckets increase minion speed by 30% and have an unlimited duration. Unlike consumable fuels they can be taken from [[Minions]] to be reused. Magma Bucket can be placed in all [[Minions]].
### Crafting

## Trivia
- Although the set doesn't grant any bonuses if the player doesn't have [[Fishing|Fishing XLV (45)]], the tiered bonus still works.
- When the set was first released, some pieces used to give more [[Magma Lord Fragment|Magma Lord Fragments]] than intended, allowing players to recraft the boots and helmet for free.
- Since [[Magma Lord Fragment|Magma Lord Fragments]] don't stack and cannot be stored in Sacks, players would need 10 inventories worth of them to be able to upgrade the full set of armor.
  - They however can be used from Ender Chest and don't necessarily need to be in player's inventory.

Tooltip: Health: +45; Strength: +10; Ferocity: +2; Sea Creature Chance: +2.5%; Tiered Bonus: Fireproof (0\ 2); Provides immunity to Crimson Isle Lava.; Tiered Bonus: Lord's Blessing (0\ 5); Grants +1 Sea Creature Chance.; Grants +20 Magic Find on  Magmatic mobs.; Damage taken from  Magmatic mobs is reduced by 15% and damage dealt is increased by 1.3x.; Right-click to equip!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Fishing Skill 45.; LEGENDARY NECKLACE
The Magma Lord Necklace is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Necklaces|Necklace]]. It is part of the [[Magma Lord Armor]] set and requires [[Fishing|Fishing XLV (45)]] to use.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|5x [[Magma Lord Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Magma Lord Fragment]] ×5 → Magma Lord Necklace|
## Usage
Necklace:
Magma Lord Necklace [[Health]]: +45 [[Strength]]: +10 [[Ferocity]]: +2 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +2.5
[Effects]
[Damage taken from Magmatic mobs is reduced by 15% and damage dealt is increased by 1.3x. Tiered Bonus: Fireproof; Provides immunity to nether magma.; Pieces Required: 2+ Tiered Bonus: Lord's Blessing; Grants +1 (4 Pieces)➜+1.5 (5 Pieces) Sea Creature Chance; Grants +20➜+30 Magic Find on Magmatic mobs; Pieces Required: 4+]
## Trivia
- Magma Lord Necklaces crafted before the 0.21.1 update did not retroactively get attributes, nor could players roll attributes on them using a [[Wheel of Fate]].
- Unlike the rest of the [[Magma Lord Armor]], the Magma Lord Necklace cannot be upgraded with [[Crimson Essence]].

The Main Street Barn Skin can be purchased from the Winter 2024 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it costs 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]].

Pet ID: MAMMOTH
Tooltip: Combat Pet; Defense: +0.5➡50; Cold Resistance: +0.1➡10; Wooly Coat Gain a 1➡100% chance for mobs to not inflict  Cold when damaging you in the Glacite Mineshafts.; Tusk Luck Gain +0.01➡0.5 Magic Find for every 100  Mining Fortune, doubled in the Glacite Tunnels and Glacite Mineshafts.; Corpse Crusher Gain +0.3➡30 Mining Fortune for each Frozen Corpse looted in your current Glacite Mineshaft.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; LEGENDARY
The Mammoth Pet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
A Mammoth Pet can be forged in [[The Forge]] with 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Onyx Gemstone]] and 1 [[Tusk Fossil]] for 7 days after donating [[Tusk Fossil]] to [[Dr. Stone]]. It requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Mammoth Pet]] 1 Mammoth Pet
  - 1 [[Tusk Fossil]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Onyx Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]]

## Tips
- Equipping a Mammoth Pet in a [[Glacite Mineshafts]] prevents the player from increasing [[Cold]] when hit by [[Glacial (Mob Type)|Glacial]] mobs.
- A Mammoth Pet grants more [[Magic Find]] than a [[Golden Dragon Pet]] at 2,100 [[Mining Fortune]] and a [[Black Cat Pet]] at 3,100 [[Mining Fortune]].
  - While a [[Golden Dragon Pet]] may start with less [[Magic Find]], its abilities provide more than a Mammoth Pet can.
  - Additionally, the only items affected by [[Magic Find]] in [[Glacite Mineshafts]] are [[Glacite Jewel|Glacite Jewels]], [[Glacite Chunk|Glacite Chunks]], [[Glacite Amalgamation|Glacite Amalgamations]] and [[Littlefoot Fluff]].

Tooltip: Power Stone Combine x9 of this item at the Thaumaturgist in the Hub to unlock the Strong power.; At 1,000 Magical Power:; +387.74&#xE00D; Strength +387.74&#xE007; Crit Damage; Unique Power Bonus:; +25&#xE00D; Strength +25&#xE007; Crit Damage; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 30.; RARE POWER STONE
The Mandraa is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Strong]] power for [[Accessories]], granting [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]]. It also provides +25 [[Strength]] and +25 [[Crit Damage]] as its unique power bonus.
## Obtaining
Mandraas are obtained from the Reward Chests in the Burning Tier, or higher, of the [[Kuudra]] fight.
Drop Chances:
- Burning Tier: 1.01%
- Fiery Tier: 1.17%
- Infernal Tier: 1.43%
## Requirements
The Mandraa has a [[Combat|Combat XXX (30)]] requirement and is only used as a [[Power Stones|Power Stone]] for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Mandraas at [[Maxwell]] will unlock the Strong power permanently.

# Mango Dye
Aliases: Mango Dye (Old)
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Mango Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_MANGO
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #FDBE02!; LEGENDARY DYE
Mango Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Mango Dye can be obtained from Tree Gifts on [[Galatea]].
|Tree Gifts|Chance|
|-|-|
|Fig Tree Gifts ([[Tree Gifts]])|0.0001% (1 in 1,000,000)|
|Mangrove Tree Gifts ([[Tree Gifts]])|0.0002% (1 in 500,000)|
|Helix Tree Gifts ([[Tree Gifts]])|0.001% (1 in 100,000)|
Mango Dye drop chance is not affected by [[Tree Gifts|Tree Gift]] modifiers (tree size, [[Attributes|Moonglade Serendipity]], etc.) and cannot be doubled with [[Karma]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] or [[Attributes|Forest Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
The drop chance of this [[Dyes|Dye]] may be temporarily doubled or tripled by [[Vincent]].
## Usage
Mango Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece that has base texture of [[Leather Armor]] by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #FDBE02.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

# Matcha Dye
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Matcha Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_MATCHA
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #74A12E!; LEGENDARY DYE
Matcha Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
A Matcha Dye can be obtained from killing any tier of the [[Revenant Horror]]. The drop chance depends on what tier of the slayer boss is killed. This chance is not affected by [[Magic Find]].
|Slayer Tier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Tier I|0.00001% (1 in 10,000,000)|
|Tier II|0.00004% (1 in 2,500,000)|
|Tier III|0.0001% (1 in 1,000,000)|
|Tier IV|0.0002% (1 in 500,000)|
|Tier V|0.0004% (1 in 250,000)|
## Usage
Matcha Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #74A12E. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

## Trivia
- The wither spawned from the Witherborn full set bonus can be teleported to by using a [[Shadow Fury]] or a [[Silent Death]].
- You can disable the Witherborn full set bonus by clicking on the key bind assigned to your "Pick Block" button in the key binds settings.

Tooltip: Hunter's Karma I Grants +1  Hunter Fortune.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 20.; LEGENDARY WATER SHARD (ID L29)
The Megalith Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Hunter's Karma]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Megalith Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Tortoise Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher and Water Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Megalith Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Hunter's Karma]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Hunter's Karma ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Hunting Fortune]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Megalith Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Megalith Shard has the following description:
- These legendary creatures are said to be as old as the mountains, their massive forms blending seamlessly with rocky landscapes.

# Midnight Dye
Aliases: Midnight Dye (Old)
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Midnight Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Item ID: DYE_MIDNIGHT
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #702670!; LEGENDARY DYE
Midnight Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Midnight Dye has a variable chance of dropping when killing [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]].
|Mob|Drop Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Werewolf]]; [[Nightmare]]; [[Jumpin' Jack]]; [[Scarecrow]]|1/1,000,000 or 0.0001%.|
|[[Bat Piñata]]; [[Mega Bat]]; [[Trick or Treater]]; [[Wither Gourd]]; [[Phantom Spirit]]; [[Scary Jerry]]; [[Wraith]]; [[Crazy Witch]]|1/500,000 or 0.0002%.|
|[[Headless Horseman]]; [[Phantom Fisher]]; [[Grim Reaper]]|1/50,000 or 0.002%.|
## Usage
Midnight Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #50216C. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

Tooltip: Mining Pet; Intelligence: +1➡100; Magic Find: +0.05➡5; Mining Speed: +1.5➡150; Archaeologist Increase your chance of finding Scavenged Items in the Mines of Divan by 0.25➡25.; Magnetic Nose Automatons drop their parts 50%; more frequently.; Nucleic Explorer Gain a 1➡100 chance to receive an extra drop when completing the Crystal Nucleus.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; LEGENDARY
The Mole Pet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mining]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
The Mole Pet can be forged in [[The Forge]] with 1 [[Claw Fossil]] and 300,000 [[Coins]] for 3 days. It requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]] to forge.
## Usage
In addition to being used as a pet, a [[Mole Pet]] Mole Pet can be donated to [[Vincent]] in exchange for their [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.
### Forging Tree
- [[Mole Pet]] 1 Mole Pet
  - 1 [[Claw Fossil]]
  - 300,000 (4,687 stacks plus 32) [[Coins]]

## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Miner Mole Skin]]|[[Miner Mole Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - April 20, 2024 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Tips
- A Mole Pet is generally better than a dedicated [[Magic Find]] pet, including a [[Golden Dragon Pet]], for [[Automaton]] drops.
  - However, [[Looting|Looting IV]] should be used rather than [[Looting|Looting V]], as the latter pushes the drop chance of robot parts from [Lv150] [[Automaton|Automatons]] to 5.25%, and drop chances of 5% or above are not affected by [[Magic Find]].
  - Specifically, a [Lv100] Mole Pet with a [[Poignant Lucky Clover]] and a weapon with [[Chimera|Chimera V]] and [[Looting|Looting IV]] is better than a [Lv200] [[Golden Dragon Pet]] with 100M [[Gold Ingot]] collection, a [[Hephaestus Relic]], and a weapon with [[Chimera|Chimera V]] and [[Looting|Looting V]] if the player has at least 5 [[Magic Find]] without a pet equipped.
## Trivia
- The Archaeologist perk's increase is added as a flat buff to the base chance of [[Scavenged Items]], meaning it buffs them from 20% to 45% at [Lv100].
- The Magnetic Nose perk's increase stacks multiplicatively with [[Looting]], both before [[Magic Find]].
## Bugs
- Magnetic Nose grants its multiplicative buff as if the Mole Pet were [Lv100] instead of scaling with its Level.

|Aug 20, 2024|Doubled [[Mining Speed]].|
|Aug 20, 2024|Reworded the description of Archaeologist.|
|Aug 20, 2024|Changed Blind as a Mole (up to 100% chance for Professor Robot to ask for 1 less part) to Magnetic Nose (Automatons drop their parts up to 50% more frequently).|

The Moltenfish Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Star Bait]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Kuudra
A Moltenfish Shard can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Loot]].
### Fusion
A Moltenfish Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Kraken Shard]]
- Second: Water Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Moltenfish Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Star Bait]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Star Bait ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +0.1%–1% chance to fish a Shinyfish ([[Shinyfish Shard]]).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Moltenfish Shard has the following description:
- This demon fish is covered in jagged, molten scales that can melt through steel, and it leaves a trail of boiling water in its wake.
## Trivia
- [[Wooden Bait]] can be used to efficiently catch [[Shinyfish Shard|Shinyfish Shards]].

The Moonbloom Mooshroom Cow Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Moonbloom Mooshroom Cow Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on August 24, 2024. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 7,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Moonbloom Mooshroom Cow Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.
- This skin adapts to the day-night cycle!
